Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in projective theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 340 and the invariant I(A,B) = 145 mod 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 340 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the projective structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 145. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
